    ratios were about 1.25e-7 for condition 100 and 4.4e-8 through 4.6e-7 for
    condition 1,000.  Patch-wide common coefficient errors could not certify
    any of the twelve roots.  Fused `CV-origin*weight` construction, fused dot
    accumulation, one-sided error-free-transform envelopes, and preservation
    of individual coefficient intervals instead of collapsing them to the
    worst patch-wide error allowed binary64 interval Krawczyk to certify both
    condition-100 candidates and one side of each condition-1,000 h/T=1e-6
    pair.  A provisional outward long-double path certified both sides of the
    latter pair on this platform.  Neither binary64 nor the 64-bit-mantissa
    long-double path certified the condition-1,000 h/T=1e-8 pair; its analytic
    chord is nevertheless 30.5 model tolerances and therefore cannot be
    dismissed as a minimum-size contact.

    Root existence is only part of publication.  The experiment also tested a
    fold-specific uniqueness argument.  If the dominant transformed equation
    is strictly monotone in the regular parameter, has opposite signs on its
    two regular boundaries, and the Bernstein polynomial for the projected
    Jacobian determinant is one-signed, the implicit regular-equation corridor
    has at most one crossing.  Krawczyk existence then makes that root unique
    in the parent.  When the parent touches the zero-determinant fold boundary,
    an interval-excluded boundary strip can be removed before applying the
    same argument.  This certified one of the two parent boxes in each hard
    condition-100 shot and, after locally conditioning the strip exclusion,
    one parent in each condition-1,000 h/T=1e-6 shot.  The opposite parents
    remained inconclusive.  Generic complement subdivision was a poor
    substitute: even a 1,024-box allowance per parent exhausted while refining
    interval ambiguity next to the certified box.

    The experimental implementation was reverted after measurement.  It had
    no exact-dyadic audit for the new Bernstein-product determinant, no
    portable extended-precision contract, no complete two-parent coverage,
    and no cross-span event deduplication.  The evidence favors a small hybrid
    path: use conditioned hulls to localize the fold, bounded scalar brackets
    to propose both one-sided roots, per-coefficient or expansion arithmetic
    for certification, and a monotonic corridor/degree proof rather than a
    dense complement tiling.  Assemble every failed certificate and threshold
    case into a C/C++ solver corpus before further arithmetic changes.  An
    uncertified case must continue to use the existing fallback.

    This further supports tractability under the stated engineering contract.
    The search and candidate solve are already bounded and reliable on the
    known failures; the open work is a finite set of enclosure, uniqueness,
    and topology proofs.  It does not make arbitrary degenerate rational
    patches with unspecified tolerance intent into a tractable exact,
    fixed-resource problem, and the implementation should not promise that.

53. The first permanent focused fold-solver corpus now ratchets bounded
    candidate recovery independently of root certification and production hit
    publication.  A private allocation-free test entry reuses the same
    conditioned derivative frame as the production rotated-hull exclusion.
    It samples 17 ordered weak-parameter values, solves the regular equation
    with at most 56 bisections, bisects each weak sign bracket at most 64
    times, and stores at most four candidates in caller-provided fixed arrays.
    Exhaustion and all work counts are explicit.  The routine only proposes
    nominal roots; coefficient-error bounds do not turn it into an existence,
    uniqueness, or completeness certificate.

    The analytic tensor-product system is

      phi(u,v) = u - 0.37
      psi(u,v) = (v - 0.5)^2 - epsilon.

    For positive epsilon, its two roots have prescribed separation
    2*sqrt(epsilon).  The permanent matrix sweeps separations 2^-4 through
    2^-24, exact contact at epsilon=0, and a strict miss at negative epsilon.
    Five exact binary equation bases cover identity, equation exchange, sign
    reversal, and diagonal/off-diagonal scale ratios of 2^200.  Five parameter
    variants cover reversal of either coordinate, exchange of u and v, and a
    combined exchange/reversal.  The result is 200 systems with 325 expected
    roots.  Candidate count, one-to-one analytic location, normalized
    residual, bracket count, the fixed 17 samples, a 147-solve theoretical
    ceiling, capacity, and frame availability are all hard gates.  The first
    optimized run found every root through 5.96046448e-8 separation with zero
    represented location/residual error and used at most 61 regular solves.

    This corpus also records an important scope boundary.  A derivative-only
    orthogonal equation frame is invariant to signed permutation and wide
    independent binary scaling, but it does not undo an arbitrary shear that
    hides a large multiple of the weak equation in the regular equation.
    Non-dyadic equation mixing can also perturb or numerically split an exact
    double root below coefficient precision.  Such cases need explicit
    input/construction-error envelopes and stronger rank-revealing or
    coefficient-aware conditioning before they can be claimed as invariants;
    loosening the root-location tolerance would hide the issue.  The
    production hull exclusion remains safe for every finite mixing matrix
    because transformed hulls have exclusion authority only when their
    outward intervals omit zero.  Candidate recovery has no such authority.

    Factoring the production frame construction into the shared helper leaves
    all prior hull decisions unchanged.  Focused optimized `--fold-only`,
    `--interval-only`, and `--affine-only` gates pass.  The ordinary affine
    baseline remains 36/36 selected, 970 surface boxes, and 446 exclusions in
    920 rotated-hull attempts; the six known grazing gaps are unchanged.  The
    next promotion step is therefore not more sampling.  It is exact-audited
    per-coefficient construction plus complete one-sided existence/uniqueness
    proofs for both hard span parents and physical cross-span deduplication.

54. The arithmetic and synthetic-proof layer needed by that next step is now
    permanent but still has no production publication authority.  A fixed
    Bernstein tensor-product operator forms interval control coefficients for
    each product in

      det(J) = phi_p psi_q - phi_q psi_p

    before subtracting them.  Derivative coefficients retain individual
    intervals.  Each product coefficient uses the exact Bernstein degree-
    elevation weights, with the rational binomial ratios themselves enclosed
    by outward interval division and multiplication.  Surface orders through
    16x16 produce determinant orders through 30x30 in caller-bounded storage;
    nonfinite arithmetic or an unsupported order is inconclusive.

    The independent oracle does not approximate those rational coefficients
    by resampling.  It accumulates their integer numerators with the exact
    binomial weights, divides once in long-double only to compare the final
    rational value, and uses dyadic input scaling.  It gates 172,800 nominal
    determinant coefficients over every input order from 2x2 through 16x16 at
    scales 2^-200, 1, and 2^200.  All 172,800 have a strict observed sign and
    enclose the exact value.  A second exact integer-interval oracle applies
    independent, coefficient-varying uncertainty through order 8x8 and gates
    9,408 additional lower/upper enclosures at the same scales.  This closes
    the missing Bernstein-product audit identified in Finding 52; it does not
    yet close the separate fused ray-plane coefficient-construction audit.

    A per-coefficient Krawczyk qualification path now also removes the fixed
    1e-6 determinant-ratio rejection from the proof decision.  The computed
    inverse is only a finite point preconditioner: Krawczyk remains valid for
    any such approximate matrix because outward intervals enclose every
    product with F and J.  Strict containment of the resulting image, not a
    condition-number guess, is authoritative.  Singular, nonfinite, or
    non-contained cases remain uncertified.  This distinction is important:
    poor conditioning may make the enclosure fail, but a fixed angular cutoff
    must not reject a proof that the enclosure actually establishes.

    The focused fold corpus adds 36 candidate-centered root boxes: both sides
    of separations 2^-4 through 2^-24 under parameter shears 1, 2^10, and
    2^20.  All 36 have strict Krawczyk inclusion.  Twenty-four lie below the
    old 1e-6 cutoff, and the smallest normalized determinant ratio is about
    5.68e-14.  The root boxes narrow in the weak direction as shear grows so
    coefficient magnitude does not swamp the small local residual.  An
    initial fixed relative width failed the larger shear cases even when their
    determinant ratio was less extreme; that useful failure shows that local
    aspect ratio and coefficient-roundoff control are part of the proof, not
    performance-only tuning.

    Each root certificate is paired with a larger one-sided corridor for the
    same analytic system.  A fixed interval test proves that phi_p excludes
    zero, phi has strict opposite signs on its two p boundaries for all q, and
    every Bernstein coefficient of det(J) has the expected side sign.  These
    three facts make the regular-equation locus continuous and single-valued
    and make psi strictly monotone along it.  Krawczyk existence in the nested
    root box therefore establishes exactly one root in each of the 36 tested
    corridors, rather than merely converging to a candidate.  Exact contact
    still has no strict determinant sign and is intentionally not certified.

    Optimized and ASan/UBSan `--fold-only` and `--interval-only` gates pass;
    the complete optimized ray/Crofton corpus again ends with
    `BREP ray correctness corpus: PASS`.  Production hit behavior is
    unchanged.  Promotion against the six real affine misses still requires
    interval-preserving fused ray-plane coefficient construction and
    restriction, proof or exclusion of the strip between each corridor and
    the fold boundary, qualification of both real span parents, and
    cross-span physical-event deduplication.

55. The real ray-plane interval plumbing and a permanent shadow fold probe
    now separate candidate discovery from the remaining precision failure.
    Each prepared span retains an outward interval for every ray-plane
    Bernstein coefficient, and those intervals follow the same outside-span
    reparameterization as the nominal coefficients.  Terminal candidate boxes
    are restricted directly from those original per-coefficient intervals;
    they are never widened into one patch-wide error first.  Centered
    homogeneous coordinates use a fused `CV-origin*weight` operation with one
    outward representable step, and the nominal ray and ray-plane dot products
    use fused accumulation.  The existing exact coefficient audit still
    passes.

    A new exact-dyadic restriction gate varies every source coefficient's
    uncertainty independently.  It covers every supported order pair, three
    quarter-grid sub-boxes, signed patterns, and the existing wide coefficient
    scales.  All 546,750 lower and 546,750 upper output bounds enclose the
    exact restricted values.  This closes the binary64 per-coefficient
    construction/restriction plumbing item; it does not establish that
    binary64 intervals are narrow enough for every grazing certificate.

    On each terminal adaptive box retained by the conditioned equation hull,
    a trace-only, allocation-free probe now runs the fixed 17-sample fold
    candidate solver.  It optionally applies at most twelve bounded nominal
    Newton steps to center the candidate, tries eight nested symmetric
    candidate boxes, restricts the original coefficient intervals directly,
    and applies the threshold-free interval Krawczyk test.  It records search,
    restriction, proof, determinant-ratio, and Krawczyk-image-excess telemetry.
    No outcome changes traversal, publishes a hit, or terminates production
    isolation.

    The result is decisive on the six known affine grazing gaps.  All twelve
    expected roots are recovered from the twelve terminal boxes, with two
    candidates and sixteen Krawczyk trials per forward/reverse hard shot, but
    none receives strict binary64 inclusion.  The condition-100 h/T=1e-8
    cases have minimum determinant ratios about 1.24e-7 to 1.26e-7 and best
    normalized image excesses 0.058 to 0.0663.  The condition-1,000 h/T=1e-6
    cases have ratios about 4.61e-7 to 4.62e-7 and excesses 0.337 to 0.365.
    At h/T=1e-8 the ratios fall to about 4.42e-8 to 4.47e-8 and the image
    excess rises to 40.9 through 41.5.  An anisotropic aspect-ratio grid and
    the nominal Newton refinement did not move this boundary; the grid was
    reverted because it only added work and reduced easier certificate
    counts.  The evidence therefore identifies enclosure precision, not root
    search, iteration count, or the old determinant threshold, as the next
    problem.

    The focused optimized and ASan/UBSan `--interval-only`, `--fold-only`, and
    `--affine-only` gates pass.  The interval audit reports 546,750 common-
    error and 546,750 per-coefficient restrictions; the fold corpus retains
    200 systems, 325 roots, 36 strict root certificates, and 36 uniqueness
    corridors.  The complete optimized ray/Cauchy-Crofton corpus ends with
    `BREP ray correctness corpus: PASS`.  Production partitions and the six
    recorded grazing gaps remain unchanged.  Fused nominal arithmetic changes
    the ordinary affine diagnostic baseline by one conservative hull decision
    to 36/36 selected, 970 surface boxes, and 445 exclusions in 919 attempts.

    This also preserves the transformation contract established by the Cobb
    and analytic sphere gates.  Translation, arbitrary rigid rotation, and
    uniform scale are classifier similarities when geometry, rays, and every
    model-space length tolerance transform together; the current directed
    Cobb classifier and sphere solver matrices pass that invariant.  Keeping
    a fixed absolute tolerance while scaling the geometry is a different
    modeling contract and is not expected to be invariant.  Nonuniform scale
    is not a similarity, so it is tested separately through the analytic
    sphere-to-ellipsoid affine oracle.  Ordinary affine rays pass through
    condition 1,000, while the six tangent-limit gaps above prevent a broader
    affine-grazing invariance claim.

    The next arithmetic step is a portable fixed-size error-free expansion or
    equivalent rigorously outward precision tier, applied only to localized
    ambiguous boxes and checked against an independent exact oracle.  It must
    not rely on platform `long double`.  After it certifies the real roots,
    both adjacent span parents still need complete corridor/fold-boundary
    coverage and cross-span physical-event deduplication before production
    promotion.

56. A portable fixed-size expansion precision tier now implements the next
    arithmetic checkpoint without changing production authority.  Each
    expansion stores at most 128 binary64 components.  Error-free `TwoSum`
    addition and an FMA residual for `TwoProduct` preserve the exact result of
    operations on the represented inputs.  Nonfinite arithmetic, a product
    whose residual could underflow below the representable binary64 expansion
    range, or capacity exhaustion returns inconclusive.  There is no heap
    allocation and no dependence on platform `long double`.

    Ray-plane coefficients are constructed as exact expansions of the
    represented operations in

      CV - origin * weight

    followed by each projected ray-plane dot product.  These bounds describe
    the exact equation defined by the stored binary64 CV, origin, and plane
    components; they do not claim that the binary64 plane-frame construction
    equals an ideal real-valued frame.  Expansion results are converted to an
    outward binary64 interval and intersected with the independently outward
    ordinary interval, so the shadow tier is never wider and cannot invent an
    exclusion or root if both arithmetic paths satisfy their contracts.

    Local restriction uses the tensor-product Bezier blossom directly.  An
    output coefficient with index i is the blossom with degree-i copies of
    the lower parameter endpoint and i copies of the upper endpoint.  This
    avoids the non-dyadic `a/b` division in a two-split restriction.  Each
    scalar equation is first normalized by an exactly represented power of
    two selected from its maximum coefficient magnitude.  Independent
    nonzero equation scaling preserves its zero set and greatly reduces
    underflow risk.  Full expansions are retained throughout the bounded
    blossom; an experiment that compacted after every interpolation was safe
    but did not improve a real certificate and was reverted.

    Expansion evaluation, derivative hulls, interval endpoint products, and
    the Krawczyk correction/remainder/image arithmetic use the same fixed
    representation.  Every expansion interval is intersected with the
    ordinary outward result.  Tight endpoint algebra did not add a real
    certificate beyond exact coefficient construction and restriction,
    indicating that the dominant remaining uncertainty is upstream geometry,
    candidate/proof-box layout, or the missing one-sided coverage theorem
    rather than the final Krawczyk additions and products.

    The permanent exact-oracle audit now reports:

      function       2,250 ordinary + 2,250 expansion checks
      derivative     324,000 + 324,000 checks, 2,250 contractions
      product        45 + 45 checks, all 45 contractions
      coefficient    162 ordinary + 108 expansion checks, all 108 contractions
      restriction    546,750 common-error + 546,750 per-coefficient
                     + 546,750 expansion checks, 544,500 contractions

    All observed expansion bounds enclose the exact oracle.  The largest
    expansion in these arithmetic gates uses four components; the product and
    coefficient gates use one.  Separate gates require conservative fallback
    for a product residual below the binary64 subnormal range and for a mixed-
    scale power-of-two normalization that cannot round-trip every nonzero
    endpoint exactly.  The synthetic fold corpus independently recertifies all
    36 roots with the expansion Krawczyk path, uses at most two components,
    and retains all 36 one-sided uniqueness corridors.

    On the six unresolved affine shots, the expansion path is available for
    all 96 attempted nested boxes with no failure.  It certifies both roots in
    each directed condition-100 h/T=1e-8 shot and one root in each directed
    condition-1,000 h/T=1e-6 shot: six of the twelve real candidates.  The
    per-shot certificate ratchet is therefore 2, 2, 1, 1, 0, 0.  The hardest
    condition-1,000 h/T=1e-8 pair remains uncertified, but its best normalized
    Krawczyk image excess falls from about 40.9--41.5 to 11.1--13.1.  Real
    expansion high-water is 17 of 128 components.  Increasing symmetric proof
    depth from eight to sixteen levels and adding a second weak-direction
    anisotropic mode produced no new certificates and were reverted.

    Exact transformed contact currently records two expansion certificates.
    Those equations are the exact equations of their represented binary64
    inputs, which may be slightly split by affine and frame construction; a
    numerical certificate must therefore not be confused with a physical
    ENTER/LEAVE interval.  Exact contact, minimum-size classification, parent
    completeness, and cross-span event identity remain separate gates.  The
    expansion path is consequently trace-only and cannot terminate traversal
    or publish a hit.

    The real affine ratchet permits a later production improvement to remove
    any of the six gaps.  While a gap exists it requires a recognized hard
    case, a nonzero expansion attempt count, complete availability, zero
    arithmetic failures, high-water below 128, the recorded certificate
    floor, and either a certificate or a strictly smaller image excess than
    binary64.  This prevents a fixed-this/broke-that precision regression
    without freezing the present miss count.

    Optimized `--interval-only`, `--fold-only`, `--affine-only`, and the full
    ray/Cauchy-Crofton corpus pass.  Focused ASan/UBSan builds pass the same
    three paths with no diagnostic; leak checking remains disabled because
    the host ptrace wrapper is incompatible with LeakSanitizer.  The full
    optimized corpus again ends with `BREP ray correctness corpus: PASS`, and
    production partitions remain unchanged.  The maximum-order blossom has
    a transient fixed stack cost on the order of 100 KiB, so stack use and
    execution cost must be profiled or moved into an existing bounded shot
    workspace before production promotion.

    This result reinforces the tractability boundary rather than closing the
    solver task.  Portable precision resolves half of the known real proof
    failures and materially contracts the rest.  More symmetric depth is not
    the next lever.  The next step is a one-sided corridor/fold-boundary strip
    proof using the conditioned regular/weak frame, with more faithful
    candidate-centered parameter geometry and, if still necessary, exact
    expansion propagation through the frame construction itself.  Both span
    parents and their intervening strips must be accounted for before the two
    observations can be deduplicated into a physical event.

57. The real one-sided corridor and fold-boundary strip theorem is now
    implemented as an allocation-free, trace-only proof.  The central
    decomposition is deliberately stronger than a grid of local root solves.
    In a candidate-conditioned equation frame, let phi be the regular
    equation and p its regular parameter.  The parent corridor first proves
    that phi_p has one strict sign and that phi has strict, opposed signs on
    the two p boundaries for every weak parameter q.  The implicit function
    is consequently one continuous p(q) graph crossing the whole weak
    interval.  The frame and the choice of p are nominal conditioning hints;
    only outward coefficient intervals authorize a proof.

    A full-corridor Bernstein sign for det(J) is still accepted when it is
    available, but it is unnecessarily strong near a fold.  The new bounded
    graph proof subdivides q, restricts the original coefficient intervals to
    each slab, and applies at most four interval-Newton contractions to phi in
    p.  It then requires one common strict determinant sign on the enclosure
    of phi=0 in every slab.  This is sufficient: psi is strictly monotone
    along the unique phi graph even if det(J) changes sign elsewhere in the
    two-dimensional corridor.  Restriction failure, nonfinite arithmetic,
    sign conflict, depth exhaustion, or fixed-workspace exhaustion is
    explicitly inconclusive.

    An important failed intermediate implementation recomputed det(J) after
    restricting and reparameterizing a very thin p interval.  Reparameterizing
    the equations multiplies the p derivatives, and hence the determinant, by
    the contracted interval width.  That artificial scale loss made a strict
    sign harder to prove as the graph enclosure improved.  The corrected path
    forms the determinant polynomial on the q slab before p contraction,
    tracks the contracted p range back in the original slab coordinates, and
    restricts that already-formed determinant polynomial to the range.  Its
    values and sign are preserved without the extra derivative scale factor.
    Power-of-two equation normalization remains a best-effort zero-set-
    preserving aid inside graph contraction, never an independent authority.

    The adjacent fold-boundary strip uses the same regular-graph contraction.
    It covers the weak interval with a fixed depth-eight binary traversal and
    proves on every contracted graph enclosure that either phi or psi has a
    coefficient hull excluding zero.  Thus no second system root lies between
    the certified corridor and its fold-side boundary.  The traversal uses a
    16-entry stack, a 511-box visit cap, fixed tensor arrays, and no allocation.
    Arithmetic, restriction, depth, and workspace failures have separate
    telemetry and retain the dense-tree fallback.  An exact-contact fixture
    intentionally reaches the depth bound and remains unexcluded, confirming
    that the path does not turn contact uncertainty into a material interval.

    The first whole-box hull chooses the regular/weak axis.  A
    candidate-Jacobian frame conditions the two equations, after which each
    real candidate tries the dyadic fractions 1/16, 1/8, 1/4, 3/8, 1/2, 5/8,
    3/4, and 7/8 between the candidate and its nearest weak boundary.  Direct
    restrictions always start from the original expansion coefficient
    intervals.  A complete shadow proof requires expansion Krawczyk existence,
    a unique one-sided corridor, and exclusion of the remaining fold strip.
    None of these outcomes yet terminates traversal or publishes a hit.

    The strengthened dyadic synthetic graph corpus has 72 corridor cases:
    six root separations, both sides, both parameter orientations, and
    independent equation scales 2^-100, 1, and 2^100.  A coupled term is large
    enough that the determinant has no strict sign on the whole corridor in
    every case.  Restriction to the contracted implicit graph recovers the
    expected strict sign in all 72, using 162 contractions.  Another 72
    root-free strip cases are all excluded, using 198 contractions, while the
    exact-contact case remains inconclusive.  The optimized fold summary is:

      graph=72/72/1 boxes=72/162+81/198 high=7

    where the first triplet is corridor/strip/contact case count and the two
    box pairs report determinant-graph and strip boxes/contractions.

    The determinant restriction itself has a new exact-dyadic enclosure gate.
    It covers every supported even determinant order from 2 through 30 in
    both tensor directions, all three half/quarter subintervals, scales
    2^-200, 1, and 2^200, plus the 30x30 maximum-order corner.  All 33,408
    checked output coefficients contain the exact restricted lower and upper
    controls.  The older complete determinant construction audits remain
    172,800 exact nominal coefficients plus 9,408 coefficient-varying interval
    cases.  This directly guards the corrected preserve-scale operation rather
    than relying only on a real-ray behavioral success.

    All six hard directed affine shots now permanently ratchet the proof path
    even if a later production fix eliminates their recorded miss.  Both
    condition-100 parents have complete existence, corridor uniqueness, and
    strip exclusion in each ray direction.  Every condition-1,000 candidate
    also has its unique corridor and both adjacent strips covered.  At h/T=
    1e-6, expansion Krawczyk existence still certifies only one of the two
    candidates per direction; at h/T=1e-8 it certifies neither.  The remaining
    per-shot complete-proof counts are therefore 2, 2, 1, 1, 0, 0 even though
    corridor and strip counts are 2/2 throughout.  This isolates the next
    blocker to candidate existence precision or upstream frame construction,
    not missing parent coverage.

    Optimized `--fold-only`, `--affine-only`, and `--interval-only` pass.  The
    independent fold and affine modes were run concurrently as separate
    processes; their optimized wall times are approximately 0.04 and 1.42
    seconds.  ASan/UBSan `--fold-only`, `--affine-only`, and `--interval-only`
    likewise pass when sharded as independent processes.  The full optimized
    corpus ends with `BREP ray correctness corpus: PASS`.  Process-level build
    and test parallelism is safe and useful now.  Shared-process parallelism
    remains a separate qualification task because OpenNURBS/model fixtures and
    global state must first be audited, and arithmetic counters must stay
    deterministic.

    Production behavior remains unchanged.  Finding 59 below supersedes the
    proposed frame-first next step by proving the remaining existence cases
    on the unique regular graph.  Only after cross-span event identity and
    minimum-segment/contact classification agree may the shadow event replace
    the dense-tree fallback.

58. A first exact-perpendicular ray-plane frame experiment was rejected and
    reverted after the new permanent ratchets exposed a trade rather than a
    monotone improvement.  The prototype selected the largest ray-direction
    component k, applied a common exact power-of-two scale, and formed the two
    algebraic normals

      n1[k] = -d[a], n1[a] = d[k]
      n2[k] = -d[b], n2[b] = d[k]

    for cyclic (k,a,b).  In exact real arithmetic both dot products with d
    vanish and n1 cross n2 equals d[k] d, avoiding the current Unitize/cross-
    product rounding.  This is still a useful construction for a future exact
    frame path.

    As a drop-in replacement, however, it reduced each directed condition-100
    expansion existence floor from two roots to one and changed several
    ordinary prepared-selector counts.  It removed the two transformed exact-
    contact expansion certificates and improved one hardest condition-1,000
    image excess from about 13.1 to 10.1, but the other remained about 11.7
    and neither hardest root certified.  The net result violated the existing
    affine ratchet and was reverted without a source change.

    This shows that exact ray-plane representation, equation conditioning, and
    candidate proof-box geometry are coupled.  Do not replace the frame on the
    strength of orthogonality alone.  A next experiment should retain the
    present floors, compare bounded exact algebraic pivot frames as shadow
    alternatives, and construct each candidate box in the selected frame's
    conditioning geometry.  Exact contact improving while ordinary roots
    regress is diagnostic evidence, not a promotion result.

59. All twelve known hard affine fold candidates now have complete shadow
    existence/uniqueness/strip proofs without changing production hit
    publication.  Two monotone additions closed the former existence gap.

    First, `brep_trace_expansion_krawczyk_contractions` turns an unsuccessful
    expansion Krawczyk image into a bounded contraction opportunity.  It
    intersects the outward image with [0,1]^2, maps the retained box outward
    into original span coordinates, restricts again from the original
    expansion-valued coefficient net, and retries at the new midpoint.  The
    loop has at most four attempts, fixed stack arrays, no allocation, and
    separate attempt/applied/empty-or-degenerate/unchanged/failure telemetry.
    A failed or unchanged contraction remains inconclusive.  This improves
    the six directed real-shot expansion certificate floors from
    2/2/1/1/0/0 to 2/2/2/2/0/0: both condition-1,000 h/T=1e-6 candidates now
    certify in each ray direction.  The h/T=1e-8 Krawczyk images are
    classified as unchanged and retain their existing 11--13 normalized
    excess; the ratchet intentionally permits that conservative outcome.

    Second, two-dimensional Krawczyk inclusion is no longer the sole
    existence theorem after a unique regular graph has been established.
    `brep_fold_graph_boundary_existence` takes the already-proved phi=0 graph
    p(q).  On q=0 and q=1 it uses bounded one-dimensional interval Newton to
    contract p around the unique phi root, restricting both expansion-valued
    boundary curves at every step.  A strict psi sign on each contracted
    endpoint enclosure, with opposite signs at the two q boundaries, proves
    existence by continuity.  The existing common strict det(J) sign on the
    graph makes psi strictly monotone and supplies uniqueness.  Exact
    singleton p contractions are evaluated directly.  A zero/uncertain psi
    endpoint, restriction/arithmetic failure, or unavailable graph remains
    inconclusive, so exact contact is not promoted to a crossing.

    The theorem is independently gated through `_rt_brep_fold_graph_test`.
    The exact-dyadic synthetic matrix reports endpoint existence available in
    all 145 cases.  It certifies all 72 root-bearing, whole-determinant-
    unsigned corridors and certifies none of the 72 root-free strips or the
    exact-contact case.  The aggregate is:

      graph=72/72/1 boxes=72/162+81/198 boundary=145/72/778 high=7

    Thus the positive and negative paths both cover axis interchange,
    separations through 2^-24, and independent equation scales 2^-100, 1,
    and 2^100.  The real affine ratchet requires two available and certified
    endpoint proofs, nonzero contractions, zero failures, both unique
    corridors, and both excluded fold strips for every hard shot.  Its
    complete-parent counts are now 2/2/2/2/2/2 even though the separately
    retained expansion Krawczyk floors are 2/2/2/2/0/0.

    This changes the next engineering boundary.  More frame precision may
    still improve numerical conditioning, but it is no longer required to
    prove these twelve roots.  The immediate solver task is to prove that the
    two adjacent-span observations denote one physical event, deduplicate
    them without erasing distinct close roots, and apply the model minimum-
    segment/exact-contact policy before any shadow result gains production
    authority.  The dense-tree fallback remains active until that work and
    its partition/Crofton gates are complete.

60. Cross-span fold events now have a conservative production path, and all
    six known tolerance-resolved affine grazing gaps are closed.

    A complete parent proof now stores one fixed-capacity fold-root record
    containing the face/span identity, nominal UV and ray parameter, localized
    UV box, rigorous outward ray-parameter interval, and determinant sign.
    `brep_trace_localize_fold_root` tries 24 dyadically shrinking slabs about
    the candidate in the weak parameter.  Every retained slab independently
    re-establishes the regular phi graph, proves endpoint existence, contracts
    the regular parameter, maps the enclosure back to the original span, and
    computes the rational ray-t hull.  The smallest certified hull is retained.
    This is fixed work with stack arrays and no shot-time allocation.  The six
    directed hard shots use 48 localization attempts apiece, certify 8--24
    nested ranges, and report zero localization failures.

    Candidate identity is topological rather than distance-only.  Two fold
    roots may pair only when they belong to the same face, lie on distinct
    Bezier spans sharing exactly one tensor-product boundary, and have
    overlapping domains in the transverse parameter.  The candidate graph
    must give both roots mutual degree one; corner and many-to-many cases are
    deliberately unsupported.  For the ray-plane frame used here the
    certified determinant sign is the sign of `(Su x Sv) dot D`; face reversal
    flips it.  A negative oriented sign is ENTERING and a positive sign is
    LEAVING.  Nominal surface normals are checked against that proof direction
    but are not used as an angular grazing threshold.  Same-direction roots
    with overlapping t hulls are duplicate observations.  Opposite directions
    give an ENTER/LEAVE material pair or LEAVE/ENTER void pair according to
    ray order.  Ambiguous topology, direction, or multiplicity falls back.

    The material-gap decision is rigorous and scale-aware.  Outward interval
    operations compute `tol->dist / |D|`, subtract the two localized t hulls,
    and use strict comparisons.  A gap whose lower bound exceeds the minimum-
    t upper bound is resolved; a gap whose upper bound is below the minimum-t
    lower bound is a subminimum contact; overlap with the decision boundary is
    tolerance-ambiguous.  `_rt_brep_fold_interval_test` and a 36-case private
    matrix permanently gate resolved, subminimum, straddling-ambiguous, and
    overlapping-contact behavior under 0.01x/10,000x model scaling and
    0.25x/16x non-unit ray-direction scaling.  Invalid intervals, zero
    direction, and negative tolerance reject.

    Production authorization is intentionally narrower than proof discovery.
    It requires exactly two terminal boxes, no ordinary Krawczyk roots, two
    complete parent/fold-strip proofs, one mutually unique resolved material
    pair, no duplicate/void/subminimum/ambiguous/unmatched event, no arithmetic,
    localization, capacity, direction, or trim failure, and two CLEAN_HIT
    classifications strictly inside the face trims.  Every local-corrector
    observation must either match a fold t hull or be a single collapsed
    observation on one of the two adjacent spans inside the certified pair
    hull.  Any unrelated local root forces fallback.  The two proven hits are
    published directly, so the legacy normal-angle grazing cleanup cannot
    erase a rigorously resolved close pair.  Near-trim fold roots are not yet
    authorized; seam/crack policy remains independent and conservative.

    The condition-100 h/T=1e-8 pair exposed why the collapsed-local rule is
    necessary: its ordinary corrector reports one root between the two
    disjoint certified t intervals.  The two condition-1,000 h/T=1e-6 and two
    h/T=1e-8 directed cases have no local roots at all.  The strict fold path
    now publishes all six analytic intervals, matches both endpoint oracles
    within the scaled model tolerance, preserves ray reversal, and reduces
    the resolved-gap count from six to zero.  The signed sweep remains
    monotone: exact contact is not published, negative clearances remain
    misses, and positive chords below the model minimum are not claimed as
    material.  Dense SurfaceTree construction and every fallback population
    remain unchanged pending the broader corpus and performance gates.

61. The monolithic BREP ray correctness executable is now process-sharded at
    its safe semantic boundaries.  CTest registers six deterministic entries:
    directed sphere/pole/grazing and similarity rays, shared-line Crofton and
    CSG fixtures, Cobb/seam and topology fixtures, exhaustive interval
    arithmetic, fold proofs, and nonuniform affine behavior.  The executable
    retains its no-argument complete-corpus behavior, and `--core-only`
    remains available as a manual combined model-work mode.  No coverage was
    removed; the split only changes CTest scheduling.

    The first four-way split reduced the isolated optimized qualification
    wall time from approximately 238 seconds to 135.91 seconds, a roughly
    43 percent reduction.  Splitting the model work further into directed,
    Crofton, and seam entries produced six tests with an aggregate wall time
    of 138.02 seconds under `ctest --parallel 6`; the exhaustive interval
    enclosure audit remained the 137.98-second critical path.  Further
    interval subdivision is intentionally deferred: it would optimize test
    plumbing rather than advance the correctness-first production design.

    The no-argument optimized executable again ends with
    `BREP ray correctness corpus: PASS`, including the complete interval,
    fold, grazing, Crofton, affine, and seam matrices.  The five sanitizer
    shards affected by model-work dispatch (`directed`, `crofton`, `seam`,
    `fold`, and `affine`) pass concurrently in 308.91 seconds with
    `halt_on_error=1` and no ASan/UBSan diagnostic.  The exhaustive interval
    sanitizer gate was already independently clean and its code path is
    unchanged by this dispatch-only split.

    This is the chosen validation parallelism boundary.  Continue using
    independent CTest processes and parallel builds.  Do not add threads
    inside the solver merely to shorten tests until OpenNURBS shared state,
    fixture ownership, BRL-CAD resources, and deterministic telemetry have an
    independent thread-safety qualification.  Correct root/event semantics
    and the intended production pipeline now take priority over further test
    runtime work.

62. The detailed plan now defines the intended production raytracer as one
    atomic proof/evidence pipeline rather than a series of local hit-cleanup
    heuristics.  A fixed-capacity per-ray completion ledger must account for
    every candidate span and terminal box as conservatively excluded,
    completely resolved regular, completely resolved boundary, resolved
    contact, or unresolved.  Unresolved is never a miss; it selects the
    whole-ray fallback before segment publication.

    The seven explicit stages are object/prep dispatch, conservative broad
    phase, complete local root isolation, trim/geometric classification,
    topology-first physical event resolution, ordered solid-state assembly,
    and atomic publication.  Newton supplies representatives but no proof
    authority.  Events reference immutable root records, identify physical
    identity through topology plus certified intervals, and carry ENTER,
    LEAVE, CONTACT, or unresolved state.  Minimum material size is decided
    only by outward comparison against `rti_tol.dist / |D|`.

    A selected prepared solid ray must prove complete coverage, a completely
    resolved event stream, valid {0,1} material state from negative infinity,
    finite endpoint provenance, and output capacity.  Any failure reruns the
    complete legacy ray during rollout; partial prepared and legacy hits are
    never spliced.  The legacy SurfaceTree is a continuity fallback and a
    comparison source, not a correctness oracle.  The implementation plan now
    has a separate certified-event/atomic-assembly phase before tree-density
    reduction and cache redesign.

63. The first completion-ledger/physical-event pipeline is implemented and
    has production authority for exactly the previously selected clean
    ordinary and cross-span fold cases.

    Every stored terminal surface box now has an explicit unresolved,
    resolved-regular, or resolved-boundary disposition.  For a
    Krawczyk-certified regular box, the outward per-coefficient ray-plane
    intervals construct the full Bernstein Jacobian determinant and require
    one strict sign over the box.  Face reversal converts that sign to a
    certified ENTER/LEAVE direction.  This reuses the exact-audited
    derivative/product/determinant arithmetic rather than trusting the
    nominal evaluated normal; disagreement is an observable unresolved
    event.

    A separate 64-entry fixed event array preserves the source box and local
    or fold root, representative t/UV, outward t interval, face/span,
    certificate type, determinant sign, trim/adjacency provenance, and event
    direction.  Clean regular boxes and the existing strict two-parent fold
    theorem populate this array without editing the source roots.  Clean
    outside-trim roots account for their mathematical box but produce no
    physical event.  Near-trim roots remain unresolved.

    The event finalizer sorts by outward t bounds, requires an alternating
    {0,1} material state from negative infinity, and classifies each
    ENTER/LEAVE gap against an outward `rti_tol.dist / |D|` interval.  A
    resolved gap is material, a strict subminimum gap is contact evidence,
    and a straddling gap is unresolved.  Direct publication is still limited
    to resolved material events or a completely certified empty ray;
    subminimum contacts await a dedicated direct-publication gate.

    `brep_build_prepared_partition` now requires one complete event ledger and
    emits its clean certified events directly into the fixed hit workspace.
    The legacy near-miss, near-hit, angle-grazing, duplicate, and assumed-sign
    cleanup passes no longer have authority over a selected prepared ray.
    Any incomplete ledger, near trim, contact ambiguity, provenance mismatch,
    state error, or capacity limit selects the complete legacy ray before
    segment output.  This narrows authority; it does not add a new root or
    seam repair.

    Permanent provenance ratchets cover six oblique sphere rays, all 30
    similarity rays, all 36 ordinary affine rays (72 certified roots), every
    selected affine grazing hit/miss, and all six hard fold pairs in both
    directions and through condition 1,000.  The sphere interior aggregate is
    `orientation=12/12`, `complete=6`, and `segments=6`; the signed sphere and
    bowed Cobb matrices require every selected prepared ray to have a complete
    event ledger (59/59 and 102/102 respectively).  The fold ratchet requires
    two resolved-boundary source boxes and two provenance-preserving events
    for every promoted pair.

    The optimized six-process CTest gate passes 6/6 in 143.38 seconds.  The
    directed, affine, seam, and shared-line Crofton/CSG tests preserve all
    prior partitions; all paired implicit/BREP fixtures retain zero differing
    lines.  The five affected ASan/UBSan shards (directed, Crofton, seam,
    fold, and affine) pass concurrently with halt-on-error in 300.17 seconds
    and no sanitizer diagnostic.  Dense SurfaceTrees, seam authorization,
    plate mode, and every fallback population are unchanged.

64. Production edge/seam eligibility no longer trusts the bounded
    correspondence sampler.  A prep-time global sufficient theorem now proves
    that the 3D edge and both lifted trims are injective and consistently
    oriented along one shared projection axis.  The old four-sample
    projection/tangent result is retained separately as diagnostic telemetry.

    Every positive-weight rational edge Bezier span is checked with exact
    binary64 expansions.  Its projected rational control polygon must be
    nondecreasing, contain a strict increase, and join the next span at an
    exactly identical projected endpoint and parameter knot.  A monotone
    rational control polygon makes the complete projected curve monotone, so
    this is a global statement rather than a collection of point samples.

    Boundary-isoparametric trims have a second exact path.  Expansions prove
    every trim control lies on the declared constant UV boundary, prove the
    varying trim coordinate monotone, and prove all corresponding rational
    surface-boundary control polygons monotone with the edge orientation.
    Nonnegative control differences with at least one strict difference allow
    zero endpoint derivatives; this is what certifies the converted sphere's
    same-face meridian seam through both poles without weakening an interval
    sign test.

    Other regular trims use outward interval arithmetic.  Each dyadic trim
    cell bounds its rational UV derivative, a padded UV hull, restricted
    rational surface partial derivatives, and the composed lifted derivative
    along the same projection.  Only a strict oriented lower bound certifies a
    cell.  Depth 24, a solid-wide 4,096-cell budget, arithmetic failure, or an
    unresolved/reversed derivative leaves the whole edge unsupported.  Cell
    count, maximum depth, exhaustion, the old screen, and the new certificate
    are separately visible in edge trace records and Cobb CSV output.

    The ordinary convex/concave/same-surface sector fixtures, nonlinear trim
    reparameterizations, all similarities, and the bowed seam continuation
    matrix retain their prior classifications.  The controlled target uses at
    most five exact correspondence cells at depth zero.  The ambiguity fixture
    is now substantially stronger: one degree-15 trim remains on the same UV
    segment and has forward values and derivatives at all four old sample
    locations, but reverses between t=0.4 and t=0.6.  The old screen accepts
    it; the global proof reaches its explicit depth-24 fallback, publishes no
    edge span/sector/closure, and leaves the legacy ray unchanged in both
    directions.  The complete optimized six-process gate passes 6/6 in
    144.49 seconds.  The five affected ASan/UBSan shards pass concurrently
    with halt-on-error in 282.39 seconds and no diagnostic; the independently
    gated exhaustive interval arithmetic remains unchanged.  The seven
    serial/parallel cache ownership cases plus rt_primitive_brep and the
    focused NIST miss, sphere-solid, and box-plate regressions pass 11/11,
    covering normal and deserialized derived-record reconstruction.  The same
    11-test matrix passes under ASan/UBSan in 12.88 seconds without a
    diagnostic.  Rebuilt ThreadSanitizer cache qualification passes all seven
    serial, parallel, identical, different, and hierarchy cases in 15.14
    seconds with no race or lock diagnostic.

    This completes correspondence identity/orientation, not geometric gap
    authorization.  The adaptive Hausdorff gap interval remains shadow-only,
    and production crack permission still uses sampled discrepancy.  The next
    seam task is decision-directed upper/lower refinement, exact-looking gap
    handling, conservative inferred/declaration policy, and an explicit
    inconclusive result at the tolerance boundary before replacing sampled
    authorization or emitting a seam event from the new physical ledger.

65. Production edge/seam gap authorization no longer trusts the fixed
    discrepancy sample.  `discrepancy_sample_authorized` retains the former
    33-point decision as trace/CSV telemetry, while
    `discrepancy_authorized` is initialized false and can be set only by the
    conservative prep-time gap proof.  Expanded edge boxes, sector
    classification, closure, and continuation therefore cannot acquire
    authority from the sample alone.

    The proof now visits every supported two-trim record whose global
    correspondence theorem passed, including seams whose fixed sample looks
    exact and seams whose sample lies beyond the model tolerance.  Each edge
    Bezier span is paired with complete, direction-consistent intervals on
    both incident trims.  Existing positive-weight rational control-hull and
    chord-deviation bounds enclose the maximum 3D edge-to-lift distance over
    every cell.  Accepted cell bounds combine by maxima over the complete
    edge and both incident faces.

    Refinement is directed by the effective edge envelope E.  A cell with a
    strict upper bound below E is complete for authorization.  A strict lower
    bound above E proves rejection; after that result, remaining cells are
    bounded without further subdivision.  A cell still straddling or touching
    E is subdivided until it decides, reaches depth 24, or has interval width
    at most max(roundoff, 0.01 E).  A valid parent bound whose refinement
    cannot proceed remains ambiguous rather than becoming permission.
    Unsupported initial arithmetic or topology is unavailable.  The existing
    solid-wide 4,096-cell budget remains fixed; exhaustion is unavailable and
    never publishes a partial proof.

    `RT_BREP_SEAM_GAP_INSIDE`, `OUTSIDE`, `AMBIGUOUS`, and `UNAVAILABLE` are
    reported independently from the old sample bit.  Both incident trims must
    be strictly inside before the record is authorized.  Either incident trim
    can prove outside; any unresolved boundary without an outside proof is
    ambiguous.  Equality is deliberately not inside.  In particular, the
    prior unset-tolerance policy may still record an envelope inferred from
    the sample, but a g=E proof straddles that envelope and does not
    self-authorize an arbitrary crack.

    The controlled g=2T metadata matrix now proves all four outcomes.  A
    1.01g declaration and a 2g declaration are strictly inside and authorize.
    Explicit zero and 0.5g declarations retain the model T floor, obtain a
    strict outside proof, and do not authorize.  An unset declaration infers
    E=g but is explicitly ambiguous and does not authorize.  In every case
    the conservative interval contains the independently sampled diagnostic.
    The sample-invisible degree-15 correspondence reversal stays unavailable,
    even though the old sample says it would authorize.

    The bowed Cobb target retains production crack repair for all declared
    1.01g cases in its 416-ray matrix.  Identity, nonlinear opposite Mobius
    trim reparameterization, translation, cyclic and oblique rotation, 0.01x
    similarity, and 10,000x similarity all prove the same target seam inside;
    the maximum controlled decision cost is 1,130 cells at depth 11.  The
    three-seam budget fixture certifies two targets, marks the third exhausted,
    and visits 1,220 target cells within the unchanged 4,096-cell solid cap.
    The complete optimized seam shard passes with zero below-envelope leaks,
    14 production repairs, no reversal inconsistency, and no manufactured
    correspondence or capacity result.

    The complete optimized six-process correctness gate passes 6/6 in 160.94
    seconds.  The five affected ASan/UBSan shards pass concurrently with
    halt-on-error in 313.89 seconds and no diagnostic.  The seven cache tests,
    rt_primitive_brep, and the focused NIST miss, sphere-solid, and box-plate
    regressions pass 11/11 normally and 11/11 under ASan/UBSan.  Rebuilt
    ThreadSanitizer cache qualification passes all seven serial, parallel,
    identical, different, and hierarchy cases in 19.99 seconds with no race
    or lock diagnostic.  This covers both normal prep and cached
    derived-record reconstruction under the new production authorization.

66. Certified manifold-edge crack continuation is now represented and
    published as an immutable two-event physical stream.  The former
    production repair still exists as the dense SurfaceTree fallback, but the
    prepared selector no longer has to manufacture a second `brep_hit` after
    cleanup for the controlled seam cases.  New certificate classes distinguish
    `SEAM_EXISTING` from `SEAM_CONTINUATION`; a separate source class identifies
    the outside-span continuation.  Each event records the edge index, source
    root, representative source box, number of boxes owned by that endpoint,
    face/span, UV, ray-parameter interval, trim/hit class, adjacency, and
    direction.  Regular and fold records explicitly carry one source box and
    no edge identity, so equal ordinal values in unrelated classification
    domains cannot silently alias the new theorem.

    The production gate is deliberately narrower than general edge event
    resolution.  It first requires complete supported surface-span traversal,
    fixed workspace success, complete local-root storage, and no previously
    published event.  A fixed scratch trace evaluates all prepared manifold
    edges.  Every nontrimmed, non-tangent CLEAN_HIT or NEAR_HIT local root is
    tried independently.  Exactly one root must identify exactly one closure
    edge through the existing sector classifier.  That edge must have global
    correspondence, a strict `RT_BREP_SEAM_GAP_INSIDE` bound, no proof or
    correspondence exhaustion, an inside material sector, and current-ray
    distance inside its authorized envelope.  Sampled discrepancy has no
    authority at this stage.

    The opposite endpoint is solved only on the incident prepared Bezier-span
    extension.  It must have the opposite direction, lie on the required side
    of the edge observation, and pass the bounded continuation certificate.
    Exactly one continuation candidate and one certified candidate are
    required.  Every isolated certificate box must contain that root, none may
    overlap the existing endpoint, the root must lie inside its outward t
    interval, and its oriented normal must be non-tangent.  Face, span, and
    adjacent-face provenance are copied from this proof rather than inferred
    later from a fabricated hit.

    Whole-ray ownership is checked before any box disposition changes.  Boxes
    containing the selected existing root become `RESOLVED_BOUNDARY`; their
    union supplies its outward t interval.  Any other local root must be
    tangent, incident to the same certified edge, and within model tolerance
    of the edge ray parameter.  Every remaining box must contain at least one
    such witness and becomes `RESOLVED_CONTACT`.  The special edge-only case is
    accepted only when there is no other local root and every retained box
    contains the existing endpoint.  Thus absence of a tangent surface root is
    not treated as missing ownership: the independently evaluated topological
    edge is the witness, while all surface work still belongs to the endpoint.
    Any extra non-tangent/off-corridor root, multiply classified box, unowned
    box/root, overflow, or ambiguous continuation leaves all source evidence
    intact and invokes the complete legacy ray.

    Only after that proof succeeds are the two event records appended.  The
    existing event retains its clean/near classification.  The continuation is
    explicitly a trimmed `CRACK_HIT` whose `closeToEdge` state is reconstructed
    consistently.  The normal event finalizer sorts the outward intervals,
    starts outside at negative infinity, requires one ENTER followed by one
    LEAVE, and applies the same outward `tol->dist / |D|` minimum-segment
    classifier used by folds.  Publication requires one resolved material
    segment, no subminimum or ambiguous contact, a final outside state, finite
    strictly ordered representative distances, successful hit reconstruction,
    and a complete two-event ledger.  Otherwise the prepared selector returns
    a structured whole-ray fallback before allocating or emitting a segment.

    The 416-ray bowed Cobb matrix now certifies sixteen paired streams and
    promotes 118 total prepared rays instead of 102.  Fourteen edge-only pairs
    reproduce all fourteen prior production crack repairs.  Two additional
    pairs own four tangent witness boxes/four tangent roots and replace the
    former legacy endpoint error; both directions agree with the implicit
    sphere to at most 2.71e-8.  Six otherwise plausible candidates contain an
    extra non-tangent or off-corridor root and remain fallback.  The other 298
    uncertified rays also retain fallback.  There are zero below-envelope
    leaks, regressions, ambiguous promotions, reversal inconsistencies,
    subminimum contacts, or state failures.  Per-ray tests independently
    reconstruct source/witness ownership from the immutable records and check
    edge identity, certificate/source classes, box dispositions, endpoint
    intervals, ENTER/LEAVE order, and analytic endpoint error.

    The optimized six-process correctness gate passes 6/6 in 153.44 seconds.
    The five affected ASan/UBSan shards pass concurrently in 282.55 seconds
    with halt-on-error and no diagnostic.  The seven cache tests,
    `rt_primitive_brep`, and focused NIST miss, sphere-solid, and box-plate
    regressions pass 11/11 normally and 11/11 under ASan/UBSan.  Rebuilt
    ThreadSanitizer cache qualification passes all seven cases in 15.49
    seconds without a race or lock diagnostic.  No SurfaceTree density or
    cache format changed.  After adding the final per-ray assertion that an
    ownership failure cannot publish an event or select production, the
    focused optimized seam shard passes in 24.89 seconds and its rebuilt
    ASan/UBSan counterpart passes in 68.94 seconds without a diagnostic.

    This checkpoint did not yet solve general edge clustering or cyclic
    vertex fans.  Direct contact-only publication, overlapping
    representations, a crack embedded in a ray with unrelated ordinary
    segments, and many-to-many edge clusters remain explicit fallback
    targets.  Finding 67 adds the first cyclic planar vertex-fan theorem.  The
    continuation certificate localizes all possible roots and gates a
    converged corrector; a future stricter interval-existence theorem can
    strengthen that endpoint without changing the event or fallback contract.

67. Exact crossings of supported closed manifold planar vertex fans now
    publish immutable physical events.  Prep constructs one record per BREP
    vertex without changing SurfaceTree density or cache serialization.  Every
    incident edge must be unique, two-trim, globally correspondence-certified,
    within its conservative edge/lift gap envelope, and free of proof or
    workspace exhaustion.  Valence must fit the fixed local-root workspace.
    Every incident face must occur on exactly two incident edges, and the
    directed face arcs must form one connected cycle.  Boundary,
    non-manifold, disconnected, repeated, inconsistently oriented, or
    capacity-limited fans are marked unsupported rather than partially used.

    Each incident edge contributes its outgoing tangent at the vertex.  Trim
    direction, trim reversal, face reversal, and the two edge endpoints decide
    the directed arc through each face.  The arc stores its full clockwise
    sweep, not merely its endpoint vectors.  This distinction is necessary at
    reentrant corners: a 270-degree material face sector and its 90-degree
    complement have the same endpoint rays, and an endpoint-only `atan2`
    would silently convexify the vertex.  Outgoing tangents must lie in their
    incident face planes and every corner must be nondegenerate.

    The initial theorem is intentionally planar.  Incident faces must pass an
    OpenNURBS plane test with a coordinate-scaled binary64 roundoff envelope,
    `4096 * DBL_EPSILON * coordinate_scale`, rather than model tolerance.  The
    first implementation used an exact-zero plane tolerance and the
    similarity matrix exposed false unsupported results after arbitrary
    rotation.  The roundoff envelope repairs representation noise without
    authorizing genuinely curved faces or turning modeling tolerance into a
    topology classifier.

    At shot time, the ray line must contain the topological vertex within a
    similarly strict coordinate-roundoff envelope.  Only after that exact
    geometric witness exists may solver observations be grouped within one
    tenth of the model distance tolerance.  A ray merely near a vertex is not
    promoted by this theorem.  The prepared cyclic link is projected onto the
    unit sphere about the ray direction.  Each stored face sweep is divided
    into pieces no larger than pi/4, rotated with Rodrigues' formula, and
    accumulated with long-double projected `atan2` terms.  The oriented
    winding satisfies

        W(D) / (2 pi) = I(+D) - I(-D).

    A winding of +1 is ENTER and -1 is LEAVE.  This handles convex and
    reentrant solid angles without depending on which incident surface Newton
    happens to return.  A direction transverse to every incident face and a
    winding close to exactly plus or minus `2 pi` are required.  Exact
    face-tangent rays, zero winding, and numerically ambiguous winding retain
    fallback; they are not assigned material by parity.

    Whole-ray ownership is again proved before mutation.  At least one
    non-tangent local surface root and terminal box must establish numerical
    hit provenance for each physical vertex.  Every observed nearby root must
    belong to an incident face, be unique on that face, agree with the winding
    direction, and be non-CRACK.  Every such root owns all matching terminal
    boxes, and every incident terminal box covering the exact vertex parameter
    must be owned.  A box without a matching root is fallback.  The cyclic
    topology may witness an incident face whose redundant surface solve is
    absent only when that face also produced no terminal box; all evidence
    that does exist must still be accounted for.  Multiple disjoint vertex
    clusters and remaining ordinary roots are accepted only when their box and
    root ownership sets are disjoint and the ordinary theorem is complete.

    After the complete proof, owned boxes become `RESOLVED_BOUNDARY` and one
    `VERTEX_FAN` event is appended at the exact topological vertex parameter.
    Its source kind and certificate are `VERTEX_FAN`, its hit class is
    `CRACK_HIT`, its vertex identity is valid, and its edge identity is
    invalid.  Existing regular,
    fold, and seam events explicitly carry an invalid vertex identity.  The
    event records one or more source boxes and a canonical source root; stream
    telemetry separately counts attempts, candidates, winding checks,
    ambiguity, certificates, events, owned boxes, and owned roots.  No disposition or
    event changes until all vertex clusters, remaining ordinary events, the
    global {0,1} state machine, outward minimum-segment proof, and fixed hit
    reconstruction succeed.  Runtime traversal and classification use only
    fixed-capacity shot workspaces; the variable-sized cyclic records are
    immutable prep data.

    The convex gate converts an ARB8 and shoots four exact mixed
    vertex-plus-regular rays in both directions.  All eight prepared vertices
    are supported; each ray owns three roots and three boxes, emits one vertex
    and one regular event, and reconstructs the legacy one-segment answer.
    An exact face-tangent line in both directions and rays displaced only 0.25
    model tolerance from the vertex remain explicit fallback.  The concave gate
    converts an exact L-shaped implicit EXTRUDE to BREP.  A single line crosses
    its reentrant top vertex and a convex bottom vertex, producing two
    disjoint vertex events, six roots, at least six boxes, and the same two
    endpoints as the implicit primitive to 1.78e-15 in both directions.

    Translation, arbitrary rotation plus translation, 0.01x uniform scale,
    and 10,000x uniform scale plus large translation repeat the L-shaped test
    in both directions with scaled model tolerances.  All twelve vertex
    records remain supported and two certified events produce one complete
    segment in every case.  Direct redundant surface-root counts vary under
    transformation, which is why the theorem relies on the certified cyclic
    topology while still requiring complete ownership of every actual root
    and terminal box.  Tests independently reconstruct incident-face, root,
    box, direction, source-count, reversal, and final-partition ownership from
    the immutable trace rather than trusting production counters.

    The optimized six-shard BREP correctness corpus passes 6/6, with the
    interval shard remaining the 138.83-second critical path.  The focused
    cache/conversion/NIST regression matrix passes 11/11.  ASan/UBSan exposed
    a latent EXTRUDE alignment defect in the new implicit oracle: four loops
    cast every sketch segment to `carc_seg` before reading its common magic,
    although line segments may be only four-byte aligned.  They now read the
    common 32-bit magic and cast only confirmed circular arcs.  The rebuilt
    seam shard passes normally in 29.31 seconds and under ASan/UBSan with
    halt-on-error in 97.59 seconds while run beside the cache gates; together
    with the four already clean
    affected shards, the sanitizer ray gate is 5/5.  The focused cache,
    primitive-conversion, and NIST regression matrix passes 11/11 normally and
    11/11 under ASan/UBSan in 19.66 seconds.  Rebuilt ThreadSanitizer cache
    qualification passes all seven serial and parallel cases in 26.13 seconds
    without a race or lock diagnostic.
    Curved differential vertex links, tolerance-near intended vertex closure,
    and non-manifold fans remain explicit future theorems rather than
    relaxations of this planar gate.  Finding 68 adds the separate exact
    interior manifold-edge theorem; non-exact tolerance-near edge/vertex
    neighborhoods remain outside both the planar vertex and exact edge gates.

68. Exact interior crossings of supported two-trim manifold edges now publish
    immutable physical events without relying on which incident surface root
    Newton retained.  The existing prep-time edge record remains the authority:
    its rational edge/lift correspondence must be globally injective and
    consistently oriented, its conservative gap calculation must finish
    without budget exhaustion, and its proof class must be strictly
    `SEAM_GAP_INSIDE`.  Sampled discrepancy remains telemetry only.

    The shot theorem first proves that the mathematical ray line contains the
    evaluated topological edge point within a coordinate-scaled binary64
    envelope,

        max(ON_ZERO_TOLERANCE,
            4096 * DBL_EPSILON * coordinate_scale).

    This exact witness is independent of model tolerance.  Points inside the
    edge envelope of either endpoint are excluded so the cyclic vertex theorem
    or whole-ray fallback retains authority.  Only after the exact witness is
    established does the declared/certified edge envelope associate redundant
    roots on the incident trimmed surfaces.

    The oriented edge cross-section now records two classifications separate
    from its older closest-offset diagnostic.  Projecting the ray across the
    edge produces `line_before_state` from the negative cross-ray direction
    and `line_after_state` from the positive direction.  The complete oriented
    material sector, including a reflex sector at a reentrant edge, decides
    OUTSIDE-to-INSIDE ENTER, INSIDE-to-OUTSIDE LEAVE, or unchanged CONTACT.
    A direction on a sector boundary or tangent to either incident face does
    not acquire a guessed direction.

    Exact witness tolerance and root association tolerance are intentionally
    distinct.  The wider authorized edge/lift envelope admits corrected roots
    whose representative t values differ slightly among trimmed
    representations or solver runs.  Every source terminal box must still
    contain the exact topological parameter within coordinate roundoff.  Thus
    a nearby independent root whose box does not contain the witness is left
    for the ordinary theorem rather than merged by geometric proximity.  Both
    unresolved boundary boxes and already Krawczyk-certified regular boxes may
    become edge sources, but a regular box is topologically reclassified only
    after the complete edge proof succeeds.

    Every associated root must belong to an incident face, match a lifted trim
    frame inside the authorized spatial envelope, be finite and non-tangent,
    and agree with the sector transition when one exists.  Multiple roots and
    boxes from adjacent surface spans are one physical event only when their
    boxes independently enclose the same exact parameter and their directions
    agree.  This includes a periodic same-surface edge whose two trims belong
    to one face.  Duplicate ownership, an unowned incident box at the witness,
    a conflicting root, unsupported topology, or any capacity/failure state
    aborts the theorem before mutation.

    All disjoint exact edge clusters are collected in one fixed-capacity
    transaction.  A transition appends one `MANIFOLD_EDGE` event at the exact
    topological parameter, with `MANIFOLD_EDGE` source kind, valid edge
    identity, invalid vertex identity, `CRACK_HIT` class, direction, canonical
    root, representative box, complete source-box count, and the union of
    source intervals.  A CONTACT appends no transition but owns its roots and
    changes its boxes to `RESOLVED_CONTACT`.  Transition boxes become
    `RESOLVED_BOUNDARY`.  Remaining ordinary roots, including strictly
    identical same-face duplicate observations, must independently own only
    regular Krawczyk boxes.  Clean outside groups are consumed without an
    event.  No trace disposition or event changes until every root and box is
    owned, the {0,1} state stream and outward minimum-size proof complete, and
    fixed-workspace hit reconstruction succeeds.

    The edge theorem runs even when all terminal boxes initially have
    Krawczyk certificates.  This matters when an exact near-trim root was
    numerically certified as a surface root but ordinary trim finalization
    cannot establish the physical topology.  Vertex-source matching was made
    consistent with this rule as well: an exact planar vertex cluster may own
    an already regular box, and one remaining ordinary root may own several
    disjoint matching regular boxes.  Neither change broadens the vertex line
    witness or winding theorem.

    The permanent exact L-shaped implicit EXTRUDE fixture supplies three edge
    line families, each shot in both directions.  The first line produces a
    convex outer ENTER and reentrant LEAVE.  The second produces two outer
    transitions plus one reentrant CONTACT, which owns its evidence but leaves
    one continuous material segment.  The third mixes one regular bottom-face
    event with one reentrant edge event.  All production endpoints match the
    analytic L profile to at most 3.55e-15.  Transverse cases also match the
    implicit EXTRUDE.  On the exact reentrant-contact line, the implicit
    primitive reports three hits and drops the final endpoint; this is a
    separate measure-zero EXTRUDE defect, so the analytic profile is the
    authoritative oracle for that one contact case.

    A separate line through the reentrant edge lies exactly on an incident
    face-sector boundary.  It observes two exact ambiguous edge encounters,
    publishes no edge event, and cannot select a prepared partition.  The
    current surface isolator reaches its conservative box-capacity fallback
    before the edge theorem; the ratchet also permits the theorem's explicit
    unresolved rejection if earlier isolation later improves.  In either
    path, no other even-looking event stream may hide the ambiguity.

    Translation, arbitrary rotation plus translation, 0.01x scale, and
    10,000x scale plus large translation repeat all three families in both
    directions with scaled model tolerances.  The classifier, event stream,
    contact result, complete source ownership, and analytic partition are
    invariant.  A converted rational sphere adds a periodic same-surface seam
    case.  An oblique line crosses the seam exactly, with four independently
    converged seam roots/four enclosing boxes, then exits at an ordinary point;
    both directions publish one edge event and one regular event and match the
    analytic sphere endpoints to at most 7.23e-9.

    The complete optimized six-shard corpus passes 6/6 in 167.30 seconds, with
    the exhaustive interval shard the 166.93-second critical path.  The five
    affected ASan/UBSan shards pass concurrently with halt-on-error in 628.08
    seconds and no diagnostic.  The focused cache, primitive-conversion, and
    NIST regression matrix passes 11/11 normally in 4.72 seconds and 11/11
    under ASan/UBSan in 14.55 seconds.  Rebuilt ThreadSanitizer cache
    qualification passes all seven serial and parallel cases in 18.28 seconds
    without a race or lock diagnostic.  No SurfaceTree density, cache format,
    or serialized prep representation changed.  After the final independent
    assertion that the unchanged-state edge-sector classification itself is
    similarity-invariant even when a redundant contact solve is absent, the
    rebuilt seam shard passes in 24.93 seconds optimized and 178.92 seconds
    under ASan/UBSan without a diagnostic.  After adding the final
    sector-boundary fallback ratchet and fail-closed exact-edge accounting,
    the rebuilt seam shard passes in 45.84 seconds optimized and 92.77 seconds
    under ASan/UBSan; the complete optimized main corpus also passes in 49.88
    seconds.

    This checkpoint resolves exact interior manifold-edge transitions,
    unchanged-state contacts, multiple clusters, mixed ordinary streams, and
    periodic same-surface representation duplication.  It does not yet
    authorize non-exact tolerance-near lines, arbitrary endpoint-moving
    gaps/overlaps, sector-boundary tangency, edge/vertex neighborhood overlap,
    or non-manifold topology.  Those remain separate proof and corpus targets;
    the narrow paired seam-continuation theorem remains unchanged.

69. A separately certified tolerance-near incident-face contact-lobe subtype
    now resolves the six non-tangent ownership failures deliberately excluded
    by Finding 66.  The endpoint-preserving bowed-Cobb matrix identifies them
    precisely on the inward-displaced face at g/T = h/T = 0.5, 0.9, and 1.0
    in both ray directions.  Each trace contains the ordinary selected outer
    endpoint and independently isolated continuation plus two clean,
    non-tangent roots on the other incident face.  Those two roots sort as
    ENTER/LEAVE inside the analytic sphere chord.  They are a tolerance-near
    incident-surface lobe within intended material, not tangent witnesses and
    not two extra solid transitions.

    The implementation leaves the original paired seam theorem first and
    unchanged.  Only when its tangent-witness ownership rejects extra non-
    tangent roots does `brep_trace_seam_contact_roots` try the new fixed-work
    subtype.  Qualification requires:

      * a ray/edge distance strictly above the coordinate-scaled exact-line
        envelope, preserving the exact manifold-edge theorem's authority;

      * an already supported global edge/lift correspondence and completed
        strict `SEAM_GAP_INSIDE` proof, with the measured discrepancy and both
        trim lifts inside the independently authorized model envelope;

      * an initially perpendicular edge cross-section,
        `abs(ray_edge_dot) <= 1e-10`;

      * exactly two other CLEAN_HIT or NEAR_HIT roots, on the other incident
        face and one span, retained by trim classification, finite and non-
        tangent, ordered ENTER then LEAVE, and strictly between the two outer
        physical representatives;

      * exact geometric mapping of the certified edge parameter to the
        contact trim, rather than equal normalized parameter speed;

      * complete single-role ownership of all roots and boxes.  Each contact
        box must be an unresolved zero-determinant box on that face/span,
        contain at least one contact root, contain the mapped trim UV, and
        contain the projected edge t in its outward interval; and

      * a nonempty union of contact-box t intervals strictly inside the outer
        endpoints and wholly inside the oriented material sector.

    The final sector condition is a whole-segment test, not two point
    classifications.  Both t-hull endpoints are projected into the certified
    edge-normal plane; a projected segment reaching the roundoff neighborhood
    of the edge is rejected.  Otherwise its direction makes one monotone
    angular sweep shorter than pi.  The code computes the signed minor turn
    with `atan2`, unwraps that sweep, and requires its complete angular range
    to stay strictly between the oriented sector boundaries.  This matters
    for reflex sectors, where two inside endpoints alone do not prove the
    chord avoids the complementary cone.
